# Genesis Architecture Manifest

Status: Proposed
Classification: Genesis Standard
Type: Authoritative Architecture Manifest
Date: 2026-07-24
Scope: Repository architecture artifacts only. Implementation code is referenced, not modified.

## Purpose

This manifest is the single entry point for the Genesis architecture knowledge base in this repository.
It registers the authoritative architecture artifacts, their governance role, and the discovery path to the supporting archive.

## Governance Rules

1. The manifest and [STATUS.md](../../STATUS.md) are the primary entry points for architectural understanding.
2. Canonical documents are registered by file and by artifact family where the archive is large.
3. No implementation code is governed directly by this manifest.
4. If a lower-level document conflicts with this manifest, the manifest and the governing standard or constitution take precedence.
5. Supporting archive collections remain discoverable through the inventory, system map, and specification map.

## Family Notes

- The detailed architecture specification archive is discoverable through [GENESIS_ARCHITECTURE_INVENTORY.md](GENESIS_ARCHITECTURE_INVENTORY.md), [GENESIS_SYSTEM_MAP.md](GENESIS_SYSTEM_MAP.md), and [SPECIFICATION_MAP.md](SPECIFICATION_MAP.md).
- The constitutional suite is frozen and forms the normative governing layer for downstream specifications.
- The GS-0001 discovery set is the current repository-specific reuse baseline for future implementation work.
